<< 'PYEOF' > /tmp/compute_d105_d111.py\nimport json, os, sys\nimport numpy as np\n\nLOWER_IS_WORSE = {'token_entropy', 'distinct_1', 'distinct_2', 'distinct_3', 'mauve'}\nBASE = '/root/autodl-tmp/distrib_canary_collapse/results'\n\nEXPERIMENTS = {\n    '0.05': 'alpha_sweep/a005_s42_fp32_v3_d080',\n    '0.10': 'alpha_sweep/a010_s42_fp32',\n    '0.25': 'alpha_sweep/a025_s42_fp32',\n    '0.30': 'alpha_sweep/a030_s42_fp32',\n    '0.35': 'alpha_sweep/a035_s42_fp32',\n    '0.40': 'alpha_sweep/a040_s42_fp32',\n    '0.45': 'alpha_sweep/a045_s42_fp32',\n    '0.50': 'alpha_sweep/a050_s42_fp32',\n    '0.60': 'alpha_sweep/a060_s42_fp32_v3_d080',\n    '0.75': 'alpha_sweep/a075_s42_fp32',\n    '1.00': 'padding_robustness/a100_s42_fp32',\n}\n\nCANARIES = ['token_entropy', 'ece']\nDOWNSTREAMS = ['distinct_1', 'distinct_2', 'distinct_3', 'mauve']\n\ndef orient(series, name):\n    s = np.array(series, dtype=float)\n    if name in LOWER_IS_WORSE:\n        s = -s\n    return s\n\ndef onset_gen_3sigma(series, name):\n    \"\"\"D105-correct: std of entire oriented series, base=s[0], thresh=base+3*sigma\"\"\"\n    s = orient(series, name)\n    base = s[0]\n    sigma = np.std(s)\n    if sigma < 1e-12:\n        return None\n    thresh = base + 3 * sigma\n    for i in range(1, len(s)):\n        if s[i] > thresh:\n            return i\n    return None\n\ndef threshold_lead_3sigma(c_series, d_series, cn, dn):\n    co = onset_gen_3sigma(c_series, cn)\n    do_val = onset_gen_3sigma(d_series, dn)\n    if co is None or do_val is None:\n        return {\n            'canary_onset': int(co) if co is not None else None,\n            'downstream_onset': int(do_val) if do_val is not None else None,\n            'lead_time': None,\n            'significant': False\n        }\n    lt = do_val - co\n    return {\n        'canary_onset': int(co),\n        'downstream_onset': int(do_val),\n        'lead_time': int(lt),\n        'significant': bool(lt > 0)\n    }\n\ndef bh_fdr(pvals, alpha=0.05):\n    n = len(pvals)\n    indexed = sorted(enumerate(pvals), key=lambda x: x[1])\n    adjusted = [0.0] * n\n    sig = [False] * n\n    prev = 1.0\n    for rank_idx in range(n - 1, -1, -1):\n        orig_idx, p = indexed[rank_idx]\n        rank = rank_idx + 1\n        adj_p = min(prev, p * n / rank)\n        adjusted[orig_idx] = adj_p\n        sig[orig_idx] = adj_p <= alpha\n        prev = adj_p\n    return adjusted, sig\n\ndef extract_series(metrics_data, metric_name):\n    return [g[metric_name] for g in metrics_data]\n\ndef process_experiment(alpha_str, exp_dir):\n    full_dir = os.path.join(BASE, exp_dir)\n    \n    # Load all_metrics.json\n    metrics_path = os.path.join(full_dir, 'all_metrics.json')\n    with open(metrics_path) as f:\n        metrics_data = json.load(f)\n    \n    # Load analysis_results.json\n    analysis_path = os.path.join(full_dir, 'analysis_results.json')\n    analysis_5m_path = os.path.join(full_dir, 'analysis', '5method_leadlag.json')\n    \n    analysis = None\n    if os.path.exists(analysis_path):\n        with open(analysis_path) as f:\n            analysis = json.load(f)\n    elif os.path.exists(analysis_5m_path):\n        with open(analysis_5m_path) as f:\n            analysis = json.load(f)\n    \n    # Build lookup from pair_details\n    pair_lookup = {}\n    if analysis and 'pair_details' in analysis:\n        for pd in analysis['pair_details']:\n            key = f\"{pd['canary']}_vs_{pd['downstream']}\"\n            pair_lookup[key] = pd['results']\n    elif analysis and 'forward' in analysis:\n        pair_lookup = analysis['forward']\n    \n    results = {}\n    pair_order = []\n    \n    for cn in CANARIES:\n        for dn in DOWNSTREAMS:\n            pair_key = f\"{cn}_vs_{dn}\"\n            pair_order.append(pair_key)\n            \n            pr = {\n                'canary': cn, 'downstream': dn,\n                'naive_peak_lag': None, 'naive_sig': False, 'naive_fwd': False,\n                'diff_peak_lag': None, 'diff_sig_fdr': False, 'diff_fwd': False,\n                'thresh_co': None, 'thresh_do': None, 'thresh_lt': None, 'thresh_sig': False,\n                'permG_sig_fdr': False,\n                'ty_pval': 1.0, 'ty_sig_raw': False, 'ty_chi2': None,\n            }\n            \n            # Get from analysis\n            if pair_key in pair_lookup:\n                r = pair_lookup[pair_key]\n                \n                # Naive\n                naive = r.get('naive_xcorr', {})\n                pr['naive_peak_lag'] = naive.get('peak_lag')\n                pr['naive_sig'] = naive.get('significant', False)\n                \n                # Diff\n                diff = r.get('diff_xcorr', {})\n                pr['diff_peak_lag'] = diff.get('peak_lag')\n                pr['diff_sig_fdr'] = diff.get('significant_fdr', diff.get('significant', False))\n                \n                # PermG\n                pg = r.get('perm_granger_fwd', r.get('perm_granger', {}))\n                pr['permG_sig_fdr'] = pg.get('significant_fdr', pg.get('significant', False))\n                \n                # TY\n                ty = r.get('ty_granger', {})\n                pr['ty_pval'] = ty.get('p_value', 1.0)\n                pr['ty_sig_raw'] = ty.get('significant', False)\n                pr['ty_chi2'] = ty.get('chi2')\n            \n            # D105: Compute 3sigma threshold onset from raw series\n            c_series = extract_series(metrics_data, cn)\n            d_series = extract_series(metrics_data, dn)\n            thresh = threshold_lead_3sigma(c_series, d_series, cn, dn)\n            pr['thresh_co'] = thresh['canary_onset']\n            pr['thresh_do'] = thresh['downstream_onset']\n            pr['thresh_lt'] = thresh['lead_time']\n            pr['thresh_sig'] = thresh['significant']\n            \n            # D111: peak_lag > 0 for naive and diff\n            pr['naive_fwd'] = bool(\n                pr['naive_sig'] and\n                pr['naive_peak_lag'] is not None and\n                pr['naive_peak_lag'] > 0\n            )\n            pr['diff_fwd'] = bool(\n                pr['diff_sig_fdr'] and\n                pr['diff_peak_lag'] is not None and\n                pr['diff_peak_lag'] > 0\n            )\n            \n            results[pair_key] = pr\n    \n    # BH FDR on TY p-values across 8 pairs\n    ty_pvals = [results[k]['ty_pval'] if results[k]['ty_pval'] is not None else 1.0 for k in pair_order]\n    adj_pvals, sig_fdr = bh_fdr(ty_pvals, alpha=0.05)\n    for i, k in enumerate(pair_order):\n        results[k]['ty_pfdr'] = adj_pvals[i]\n        results[k]['ty_sig_fdr'] = sig_fdr[i]\n    \n    # Count per method\n    counts = {\n        'naive': sum(results[k]['naive_fwd'] for k in pair_order),\n        'diff': sum(results[k]['diff_fwd'] for k in pair_order),\n        'threshold': sum(results[k]['thresh_sig'] for k in pair_order),\n        'perm_granger': sum(results[k]['permG_sig_fdr'] for k in pair_order),\n        'ty_granger': sum(results[k]['ty_sig_fdr'] for k in pair_order),\n    }\n    \n    # Consensus per pair\n    consensus = {}\n    for k in pair_order:\n        r = results[k]\n        votes = sum([\n            int(r['naive_fwd']),\n            int(r['diff_fwd']),\n            int(r['thresh_sig']),\n            int(r['permG_sig_fdr']),\n            int(r['ty_sig_fdr']),\n        ])\n        consensus[k] = votes\n    \n    k_vals = list(consensus.values())\n    \n    return {\n        'alpha': alpha_str,\n        'n_gen': len(metrics_data),\n        'pairs': results,\n        'counts': counts,\n        'consensus': consensus,\n        'K_ge3': sum(1 for v in k_vals if v >= 3),\n        'K_ge4': sum(1 for v in k_vals if v >= 4),\n    }\n\n# Process all\nall_results = {}\nfor alpha_str in sorted(EXPERIMENTS.keys()):\n    exp_dir = EXPERIMENTS[alpha_str]\n    try:\n        result = process_experiment(alpha_str, exp_dir)\n        all_results[alpha_str] = result\n        print(f\"OK alpha={alpha_str}: naive={result['counts']['naive']}/8 diff={result['counts']['diff']}/8 thresh={result['counts']['threshold']}/8 permG={result['counts']['perm_granger']}/8 TY={result['counts']['ty_granger']}/8 K>=3={result['K_ge3']} K>=4={result['K_ge4']}\", file=sys.stderr)\n    except Exception as e:\n        all_results[alpha_str] = {'error': str(e)}\n        print(f\"ERR alpha={alpha_str}: {e}\", file=sys.stderr)\n\n# Write full output\nwith open('/tmp/d105_d111_results.json', 'w') as f:\n    json.dump(all_results, f, indent=2, default=str)\n\nprint(\"DONE: /tmp/d105_d111_results.json\", file=sys.stderr)\nPYEOF\necho \"Script written OK\""},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":1,"cache_creation_input_tokens":4751,"cache_read_input_tokens":41510,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":4751},"output_tokens":8,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"d1dedad6-d763-4570-8bc0-74b69b0c05e8","uuid":"2c6c15fd-f09e-4678-8582-f3996caf7d06"}

distinct_2, distinct_3, mauve}。\\n\\n**Step 2**: 对每个 pair 提取：\\n- `naive_xcorr`: peak_lag 和 significant (bool)\\n- `diff_in_diff_xcorr`: peak_lag 和 significant_fdr (bool)  \\n- `threshold_onset`: significant_3sigma 字段（如果存在）。如果不存在，需要重新计算——检查 `onset_gen` 和 `z_score` 字段。3σ 阈值 = base + 3*sigma。如果 z_score > 3.0 或者有 `significant_3sigma` 字段为 true，则算 significant。\\n- `permutation_granger`: significant (bool) 和 direction (fwd)\\n- `toda_yamamoto`: significant_fdr (bool)\\n\\n**Step 3**: 应用 D111 方向检查：\\n- naive_fwd: significant=true AND peak_lag > 0\\n- diff_fwd: significant_fdr=true AND peak_lag > 0\\n\\n**Step 4**: 对 threshold_onset 应用 D105 3σ：\\n- 如果 JSON 中有 `significant_3sigma` 字段，直接用\\n- 如果没有，检查 `z_score` 字段：z_score > 3.0 则 significant_3sigma=true\\n- 如果两者都没有，需要从 `onset_gen`, `baseline_mean`, `baseline_std` 重新计算\\n- **关键**: 服务器上的 `method_ablation.py` 已经修复为 3σ。如果分析文件是在修复后生成的，`significant` 字段就已经是 3σ。如果是修复前生成的，需要看有没有 `significant_3sigma` 字段。\\n\\n**Step 5**: 对 TY Granger 检查 FDR 校正：\\n- 使用 `significant_fdr` 字段（如果存在）\\n- 如果只有 `significant`（无 FDR），那么该值是 pre-FDR，需要标注\\n\\n**Step 6**: 计算每个 pair 的 K = count(naive_fwd, diff_fwd, threshold_3sigma, permG_fwd, TY_fdr)\\n- K≥3: 该 pair 有 3+ 方法一致\\n- K≥4: 该 pair 有 4+ 方法一致\\n\\n**Step 7**: 汇总每个 α 的 X/8 计数\\n\\n### 输出格式\\n\\n在本地 artifacts 目录写两个文件：\\n\\n1. a075_s43/s44 实验，导致 ~14 GPU-hours 浪费。根因是另一 session 未检查实验进度就执行了 kill + rm -rf + 重启。）\\n- **C005**: EXIT_CODE=129 (SIGHUP) 不等于实验失败——必须先检查 gen 目录和 all_metrics.json 完整性再判定（原因：M015: 连续三次误判（C4 α=0.50、α=0.60、GPT-2m s43），浪费诊断 Worker + GPU 空转时间） [来自 M015]\\n- **C006**: D114: Onset [0.45, 0.50]（a045=0/8 确认）。但 D093 padding 混杂未排除——a050_postd093 验证中，验证前论文 onset 写 [0.45, 0.50] 但 limitations 必须提及 D093 混杂（原因：所有 α≤0.45 为 post-D093（right padding），所有 α=0.50 为 pre-D093（left padding），boundary 与 D093 变更完全重合。三 seed 一致性降低混杂概率但无法排除。） [来自 D114]\\n- **C007**: M031: 提交实验前必须 df -h 确认目标服务器剩余空间 ≥ 实验预估大小 2x。大模型(≥3B)优先分配到大容量服务器。19828 仅 250G 总容量，谨慎使用（原因：smollm3_s43 因 19828 磁盘满失败（250G/250G），4.21h GPU 时间浪费） [来自 M031]\\n\\n\\n## 环境搭建\\n连接服务器后先调 `detect_image` 检测是否为预置镜像。如果匹配，跳过基础环境搭建，只装项目特有依赖。已预装的包不要重复安装。\\n\\n## 输出风格\\n回复简洁直接。只输出执行结果和必要信息，不要解释过程、不要客套、不要给未被要求的建议。\\n&lt;/project_context>\\n## 任务：为 12 个 α 值生成 D105+D111 完全修正的 5-method 计数表\\n\\n### 背景\\n论文的 dose-response CSV 需要每个 α 值的 5 个方法计数（naive_fwd, diff_fwd, threshold_fwd, permG_fwd, TY_fwd）以及 K≥3, K≥4 共识计数。\\n\\n有两层修正：\\n1. **D105 (true 3σ)**: `method_ablation.py` 的 `_onset_gen` 已修复为 3σ（之前是 2σ）。threshold_onset 显著性阈值从 2σ 变为 3σ。同时 FDR 按 per-method BH 校正。TY Granger 也受 FDR 影响。\\n2. **D111 (direction check)**: naive_xcorr 和 diff_xcorr 必须 peak_lag > 0 才算 forward direction。\\n\\n### 12 个实验及服务器路径\\n\\n**westd-17855** 上的实验 (`/root/autodl-tmp/distrib_canary_collapse/results/`):\\n1. α=0.05: `alpha_sweep/a005_s42_fp32_v3_d080/`\\n2. α=0.10: `alpha_sweep/a010_s42_fp32/`\\n3. α=0.25: `alpha_sweep/a025_s42_fp32/`\\n4. α=0.30: `alpha_sweep/a030_s42_fp32/`\\n5. α=0.35: `alpha_sweep/a035_s42_fp32/`\\n6. α=0.40: `alpha_sweep/a040_s42_fp32/` (注意 v2 可能在此路径)\\n7. α=0.45: `alpha_sweep/a045_s42_fp32/`\\n8. α=0.50: `alpha_sweep/a050_s42_fp32/`\\n9. α=0.60: `alpha_sweep/a060_s42_fp32_v3_d080/`\\n10. α=0.75: `alpha_sweep/a075_s42_fp32/`\\n11. α=1.00: `padding_robustness/a100_s42_fp32/` (**必须用这个路径，不是 method_ablation**)\\n\\n**westd-19828** 上：\\n12. α=0.00: `alpha_sweep/a000_s42/analysis/` (**用 analysis/ 子目录，不是 analysis_mauve/**)\\n\\n### 需要做的事\\n\\n对每个实验：\\n\\n**Step 1**: 找到该实验的 per-pair 5method_leadlag.json（通常在 `analysis/5method_leadlag.json`）。读取 8 个 pair 的数据。8 个 pair 是 canary={token_entropy, ece} × downstream={distinct_1, distinct_2, distinct_3, mauve}。\\n\\n**Step 2**: 对每个 pair 提取：\\n- `naive_xcorr`: peak_lag 和 significant (bool)\\n- `diff_in_diff_xcorr`: peak_lag 和 significant_fdr (bool)  \\n- `threshold_onset`: significant_3sigma 字段（如果存在）。如果不存在，需要重新计算——检查 `onset_gen` 和 `z_score` 字段。3σ 阈值 = base + 3*sigma。如果 z_score > 3.0 或者有 `significant_3sigma` 字段为 true，则算 significant。\\n- `permutation_granger`: significant (bool) 和 direction (fwd)\\n- `toda_yamamoto`: significant_fdr (bool)\\n\\n**Step 3**: 应用 D111 方向检查：\\n- naive_fwd: significant=true AND peak_lag > 0\\n- diff_fwd: significant_fdr=true AND peak_lag > 0\\n\\n**Step 4**: 对 threshold_onset 应用 D105 3σ：\\n- 如果 JSON 中有 `significant_3sigma` 字段，直接用\\n- 如果没有，检查 `z_score` 字段：z_score > 3.0 则 significant_3sigma=true\\n- 如果两者都没有，需要从 `onset_gen`, `baseline_mean`, `baseline_std` 重新计算\\n- **关键**: 服务器上的 `method_ablation.py` 已经修复为 3σ。如果分析文件是在修复后生成的，`significant` 字段就已经是 3σ。如果是修复前生成的，需要看有没有 `significant_3sigma` 字段。\\n\\n**Step 5**: 对 TY Granger 检查 FDR 校正：\\n- 使用 `significant_fdr` 字段（如果存在）\\n- 如果只有 `significant`（无 FDR），那么该值是 pre-FDR，需要标注\\n\\n**Step 6**: 计算每个 pair 的 K = count(naive_fwd, diff_fwd, threshold_3sigma, permG_fwd, TY_fdr)\\n- K≥3: 该 pair 有 3+ 方法一致\\n- K≥4: 该 pair 有 4+ 方法一致\\n\\n**Step 7**: 汇总每个 α 的 X/8 计数\\n\\n### 输出格式\\n\\n在本地 artifacts 目录写两个文件：\\n\\n1. `/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0514_4/projects/distrib_canary_collapse/artifacts/dose_response_5method_regime.csv`:\\n```\\nalpha,permG_fwd,K_ge3,K_ge4,naive_fwd,diff_fwd,threshold_fwd,ty_fwd,regime\\n0.00,X/8,X/8,X/8,X/8,X/8,X/8,X/8,blind_zone\\n...\\n```\\n\\n2. `/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0514_4/projects/distrib_canary_collapse/artifacts/consensus_heatmap_data.csv`:\\n```\\nalpha,naive_fwd,diff_fwd,threshold_fwd,permG_fwd,ty_fwd,K_ge3,K_ge4,regime\\n0.00,X/8,X/8,X/8,X/8,X/8,X/8,X/8,blind_zone\\n...\\n```\\n\\nRegime 分配：\\n- blind_zone: α ≤ 0.45 (PermG = 0/8)\\n- onset: α = 0.50 (PermG 从 0 跳到 6/8)\\n- sync_collapse: α = 0.60 (PermG 降到 3/8 但 K≥3 = 6/8)\\n- detection: α ≥ 0.75 (PermG ≥ 6/8)\\n\\n### 验证\\n\\n用 Director 提供的已验证参考值交叉检验（这些是 D105+D111 后的权威值）：\\n\\n| α | naive | diff | threshold | permG | TY | K≥3 | K≥4 |\\n|---|-------|------|-----------|-------|-----|-----|-----|\\n| 0.00 | 6/8 | 0/8 | 3/8 | 0/8 | 1/8 | 1/8 | 0/8 |\\n| 0.05 | 3/8 | 0/8 | 0/8 | 0/8 | 0/8 | 0/8 | 0/8 |\\n| 0.10 | 4/8 | 1/8 | 2/8 | 0/8 | 0/8 | 2/8 | 0/8 |\\n| 0.25 | 5/8 | 2/8 | 2/8 | 0/8 | 0/8 | 2/8 | 0/8 |\\n| 0.35 | 8/8 | 0/8 | 4/8 | 0/8 | 0/8 | 4/8 | 0/8 |\\n| 1.00 | 0/8 | 0/8 | 8/8 | 6/8 | 6/8 | 6/8 | 0/8 |\\n\\n如果你的计算结果与上述参考值不一致，停下来检查原因，不要直接覆盖。输出每个不一致的详细对比。\\n\\n### 同时输出 per-pair 详细审计表\\n\\n写到 `/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0514_4/projects/distrib_canary_collapse/artifacts/d105_d111_complete_audit.md`\\n\\n包含每个 α 的 8-pair 详细表：\\n| pair | naive_lag | naive_sig | naive_fwd | diff_lag | diff_sig_fdr | diff_fwd | thresh_3σ | permG | TY_fdr | K |\\n\\n这样 Reviewer 可以逐 pair 验证。\\n\\n### 注意事项\\n- 连接服务器用 ssh_read_only，不要修改任何文件\\n- α=0.00 在 westd-19828，其他在 westd-17855\\n- 如果某个实验的 analysis 目录不存在，尝试 `analysis_mauve/` 作为 fallback\\n- 对于 threshold_onset，优先找 `significant_3sigma` 或 `z_score` 字段。如果这些都不存在，检查 `onset_zscore` 或 `threshold_zscore` 等变体字段名\\n- PermG 只看 direction=fwd 且 significant=true\\n- 输出所有数值为 X/8 格式（如 α=1.00: `padding_robustness/a100_s42_fp32/` (**必须用这个路径，不是 method_ablation**)\\n\\n**westd-19828** 上：\\n12. α=0.00: `alpha_sweep/a000_s42/analysis/` (**用 analysis/ 子目录，不是 analysis_mauve/**)\\n\\n### 需要做的事\\n\\n对每个实验：\\n\\n**Step 1**: 找到该实验的 per-pair 5method_leadlag.json（通常在 `analysis/5method_leadlag.json`）。读取 8 个 pair 的数据。8 个 pair 是 canary={token_entropy, ece} × downstream={distinct_1, distinct_2, distinct_3, mauve}。\\n\\n**Step 2**: 对每个 pair 提取：\\n- `naive_xcorr`: peak_lag 和 significant (bool)\\n- `diff_in_diff_xcorr`: peak_lag 和 significant_fdr (bool)  \\n- `threshold_onset`: significant_3sigma 字段（如果存在）。如果不存在，需要重新计算——检查 `onset_gen` 和 `z_score` 字段。3σ 阈值 = base + 3*sigma。如果 z_score > 3.0 或者有 `significant_3sigma` 字段为 true，则算 significant。\\n- `permutation_granger`: significant (bool) 和 direction (fwd)\\n- `toda_yamamoto`: significant_fdr (bool)\\n\\n**Step 3**: 应用 D111 方向检查：\\n- naive_fwd: significant=true AND peak_lag > 0\\n- diff_fwd: significant_fdr=true AND peak_lag > 0\\n\\n**Step 4**: 对 threshold_onset 应用 D105 3σ：\\n- 如果 JSON 中有 `significant_3sigma` 字段，直接用\\n- 如果没有，检查 `z_score` 字段：z_score > 3.0 则 significant_3sigma=true\\n- 如果两者都没有，需要从 `onset_gen`, `baseline_mean`, `baseline_std` 重新计算\\n- **关键**: 服务器上的 `method_ablation.py` 已经修复为 3σ。如果分析文件是在修复后生成的，`significant` 字段就已经是 3σ。如果是修复前生成的，需要看有没有 `significant_3sigma` 字段。\\n\\n**Step 5**: 对 TY Granger 检查 FDR 校正：\\n- 使用 `significant_fdr` 字段（如果存在）\\n- 如果只有 `significant`（无 FDR），那么该值是 pre-FDR，需要标注\\n\\n**Step 6**: 计算每个 pair 的 K = count(naive_fwd, diff_fwd, threshold_3sigma, permG_fwd, TY_fdr)\\n- K≥3: 该 pair 有 3+ 方法一致\\n- K≥4: 该 pair 有 4+ 方法一致\\n\\n**Step 7**: 汇总每个 α 的 X/8 计数\\n\\n### 输出格式\\n\\n在本地 artifacts 目录写两个文件：\\n\\n1. `/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0514_4/projects/distrib_canary_collapse/artifacts/dose_response_5method_regime.csv`:\\n```\\nalpha,permG_fwd,K_ge3,K_ge4,naive_fwd,diff_fwd,threshold_fwd,ty_fwd,regime\\n0.00,X/8,X/8,X/8,X/8,X/8,X/8,X/8,blind_zone\\n...\\n```\\n\\n2. Summary\\n    print(f\\\"\\\\n\\\\n{'='*120}\\\")\\n    print(\\\"D105 SUMMARY: 3sigma recompute (fdr_permethod)\\\")\\n    print(f\\\"{'='*120}\\\")\\n    print(f\\\"{'Experiment':<42} {'PermG_f':>7} {'PermG_r':>7} {'K>=4(3s)':>9} {'K>=3(3s)':>9} {'Thr(3s)':>8} {'Thr(2s)':>8} {'Change':>8}\\\")\\n    print(f\\\"{'-'*120}\\\")\\n    for exp_id in [\\\"scale_69b_a100_s42\\\", \\\"qwen35_08b_wiki_a100_s42_v2\\\", \\\"padding_robustness_a100_s42_fp32\\\",\\n                    \\\"generalize_pythia410m_wiki_a100_s42\\\", \\\"gen_wiki_gpt2m_a100_s42_v4\\\",\\n                    \\\"gen_gpt2m_a100_s43_fp32\\\", \\\"generalize_opt1b_wiki_a100_s42\\\"]:\\n        if exp_id not in all_results:\\n            continue\\n        r = all_results[exp_id]\\n        delta = r['threshold_3sigma'] - r['threshold_2sigma']\\n        print(f\\\"{exp_id:<42} {r['perm_granger_fwd']:>2}/8    {r['perm_granger_rev']:>2}/8    \\\"\\n              f\\\"{r['k4_3sigma']:>2}/8      {r['k3_3sigma']:>2}/8      \\\"\\n              f\\\"{r['threshold_3sigma']:>2}/8     {r['threshold_2sigma']:>2}/8     {delta:>+3}\\\")\\n\\n    # This recompute uses true 3sigma.\\\",\\n            },\\n            \\\"results\\\": save_data,\\n        }, f, indent=2, cls=NumpyEncoder)\\n    print(f\\\"\\\\nSaved: {save_path}\\\")\\n\\n\\nif __name__ == \\\"__main__\\\":\\n    main()\\n\", \"stderr\": \"\", \"return_code\": 0}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {\"elapsed_seconds\": 0.51}}"}}}

\\\"def threshold_onset(c, d, cn, dn, sigma_mult=3):\\\\n    x, y = orient(c, cn), orient(d, dn)\\\\n    if np.any(np.isnan(x)) or np.any(np.isnan(y)):\\\\n        return {\\\\\\\"method\\\\\\\": \\\\\\\"threshold_onset\\\\\\\", \\\\\\\"onset_canary\\\\\\\": None,\\\\n                \\\\\\\"onset_downstream\\\\\\\": None, \\\\\\\"lead_time\\\\\\\": None, \\\\\\\"significant\\\\\\\": False,\\\\n                \\\\\\\"note\\\\\\\": \\\\\\\"NaN in data\\\\\\\"}\\\\n    gen0_c, gen0_d = x[0], y[0]\\\\n    std_c = np.std(np.diff(x)) if len(x) > 1 else 1e-12\\\\n    std_d = np.std(np.diff(y)) if len(y) > 1 else 1e-12\\\\n    if std_c < 1e-12:\\\\n        std_c = abs(gen0_c) * 0.01 if abs(gen0_c) > 1e-12 else 1e-6\\\\n    if std_d < 1e-12:\\\\n        std_d = abs(gen0_d) * 0.01 if abs(gen0_d) > 1e-12 else 1e-6\\\\n    thresh_c = gen0_c + sigma_mult * std_c\\\\n    thresh_d = gen0_d + sigma_mult * std_d\\\\n    onset_c, onset_d = None, None\\\\n    for i in range(1, len(x)):\\\\n        if onset_c is None and x[i] > thresh_c:\\\\n            onset_c = i\\\\n        if onset_d is None and y[i] > thresh_d:\\\\n            onset_d = i\\\\n    if onset_c is not None and onset_d is not None:\\\\n        lead = onset_d - onset_c\\\\n        significant = lead > 0\\\\n    elif onset_c is not None and onset_d is None:\\\\n        lead = len(x) - onset_c\\\\n        significant = True\\\\n    else:\\\\n        lead = 0\\\\n        significant = False\\\\n    return {\\\\n        \\\\\\\"method\\\\\\\": \\\\\\\"threshold_onset\\\\\\\",\\\\n        \\\\\\\"onset_canary\\\\\\\": onset_c,\\\\n        \\\\\\\"onset_downstream\\\\\\\": onset_d,\\\\n        \\\\\\\"lead_time\\\\\\\": int(lead) if lead is not None else None,\\\\n        \\\\\\\"significant\\\\\\\": significant,\\\\n    }\\\\n\\\\n\\\\n# ── Method 4: Permutation Granger ──\\\\n\\\\ndef _build_lag_matrix(y, x, max_lag):\\\\n    T = len(y)\\\\n    n = T - max_lag\\\\n    Y = y[max_lag:]\\\\n    cols_r = [np.ones(n)]\\\\n    for lag in range(1, max_lag + 1):\\\\n        cols_r.append(y[max_lag - lag: T - lag])\\\\n    cols_u = list(cols_r)\\\\n    for lag in range(1, max_lag + 1):\\\\n        cols_u.append(x[max_lag - lag: T - lag])\\\\n    return Y, np.column_stack(cols_r), np.column_stack(cols_u)\\\\n\\\\n\\\\ndef _granger_f_stat(y, x, max_lag):\\\\n    Y, X_r, X_u = _build_lag_matrix(y, x, max_lag)\\\\n    n = len(Y)\\\\n    q = max_lag\\\\n    try:\\\\n        beta_r = np.linalg.lstsq(X_r, Y, rcond=None)[0]\\\\n        beta_u = np.linalg.lstsq(X_u, Y, rcond=None)[0]\\\\n    except np.linalg.LinAlgError:\\\\n        return np.nan\\\\n    rss_r = np.sum((Y - X_r @ beta_r) ** 2)\\\\n    rss_u = np.sum((Y - X_u @ beta_u) ** 2)\\\\n    k_u = X_u.shape[1]\\\\n    df_resid = n - k_u\\\\n    if df_resid <= 0 or rss_u < 1e-15:\\\\n        return np.nan\\\\n    return ((rss_r - rss_u) / q) / (rss_u / df_resid)\\\\n\\\\n\\\\ndef perm_granger(x, y, max_lag=1, n_perm=N_PERM, seed=42, difference=True):\\\\n    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=float)\\\\n    y = np.asarray(y, dtype=float)\\\\n    if np.any(np.isnan(x)) or np.any(np.isnan(y)):\\\\n        return {\\\\\\\"method\\\\\\\": \\\\\\\"perm_granger\\\\\\\", \\\\\\\"f_stat\\\\\\\": None, \\\\\\\"p_value\\\\\\\": None,\\\\n                \\\\\\\"significant\\\\\\\": False, \\\\\\\"note\\\\\\\": \\\\\\\"NaN in data\\\\\\\"}\\\\n    if difference:\\\\n        x, y = np.diff(x), np.diff(y)\\\\n    T = len(x)\\\\n    if T <= max_lag + 2:\\\\n        return {\\\\\\\"method\\\\\\\": \\\\\\\"perm_granger\\\\\\\", \\\\\\\"f_stat\\\\\\\": None, \\\\\\\"p_value\\\\\\\": None,\\\\n                \\\\\\\"significant\\\\\\\": False, \\\\\\\"note\\\\\\\": f\\\\\\\"T={T} too short\\\\\\\"}\\\\n    f_obs = _granger_f_stat(y, x, max_lag)\\\\n    if np.isnan(f_obs):\\\\n        return {\\\\\\\"method\\\\\\\": \\\\\\\"perm_granger\\\\\\\", \\\\\\\"f_stat\\\\\\\": None, \\\\\\\"p_value\\\\\\\": None,\\\\n                \\\\\\\"significant\\\\\\\": False, \\\\\\\"note\\\\\\\": \\\\\\\"F-stat failed\\\\\\\"}\\\\n    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)\\\\n    null_f = np.empty(n_perm)\\\\n    for i in range(n_perm):\\\\n        null_f[i] = _granger_f_stat(y, rng.permutation(x), max_lag)\\\\n    valid = null_f[~np.isnan(null_f)]\\\\n    if len(valid) == 0:\\\\n        return {\\\\\\\"method\\\\\\\": \\\\\\\"perm_granger\\\\\\\", \\\\\\\"f_stat\\\\\\\": round(float(f_obs), 4),\\\\n                \\\\\\\"p_value\\\\\\\": None, \\\\\\\"significant\\\\\\\": False}\\\\n    p = (np.sum(valid >= f_obs) + 1) / (len(valid) + 1)\\\\n    return {\\\\n        \\\\\\\"method\\\\\\\": \\\\\\\"perm_granger\\\\\\\",\\\\n        \\\\\\\"f_stat\\\\\\\": round(float(f_obs), 4),\\\\n        \\\\\\\"p_value\\\\\\\": round(float(p), 6),\\\\n        \\\\\\\"significant\\\\\\\": bool(p < ALPHA_SIG),\\\\n    }\\\\n\\\\n\\\\n# ── Method 5: Toda-Yamamoto Granger ──\\\\n\\\\ndef _determine_d_max(series, max_d=2, alpha=0.05):\\\\n    for d in range(max_d + 1):\\\\n        try:\\\\n            result = adfuller(series, maxlag=min(3, len(series) // 3 - return {\\\\\\\"method\\\\\\\": \\\\\\\"threshold_onset\\\\\\\", \\\\\\\"onset_canary\\\\\\\": None,\\\\n                \\\\\\\"onset_downstream\\\\\\\": None, \\\\\\\"lead_time\\\\\\\": None, \\\\\\\"significant\\\\\\\": False,\\\\n                \\\\\\\"note\\\\\\\": \\\\\\\"NaN in data\\\\\\\"}\\\\n    gen0_c, gen0_d = x[0], y[0]\\\\n    std_c = np.std(np.diff(x)) if len(x) > 1 else for i in range(1, len(x)):\\\\n        if onset_c is None and x[i] > thresh_c:\\\\n            onset_c = i\\\\n        if onset_d is None and y[i] > thresh_d:\\\\n            onset_d = i\\\\n    if \\\\\\\"significant\\\\\\\": significant,\\\\n    }\\\\n\\\\n\\\\n# ── Method 4: Permutation Granger ──\\\\n\\\\ndef _build_lag_matrix(y, x, max_lag):\\\\n    T = len(y)\\\\n    n = T - max_lag\\\\n    Y = y[max_lag:]\\\\n    cols_r = [np.ones(n)]\\\\n    for lag in range(1, max_lag + 1):\\\\n        cols_r.append(y[max_lag - lag: T - lag])\\\\n    cols_u = list(cols_r)\\\\n    for lag in range(1, X_u = _build_lag_matrix(y, x, max_lag)\\\\n    n = len(Y)\\\\n    q = max_lag\\\\n    try:\\\\n        beta_r = np.linalg.lstsq(X_r, Y, rcond=None)[0]\\\\n        beta_u = np.linalg.lstsq(X_u, Y, rcond=None)[0]\\\\n    except np.linalg.LinAlgError:\\\\n        return np.nan\\\\n    rss_r = np.sum((Y - X_r @ beta_r) ** 2)\\\\n    rss_u = np.sum((Y - X_u @ beta_u) ** 2)\\\\n    k_u = f\\\"detection_rate={mmct_result.get('detection_rate', 'N/A')}\\\")\\n\\n    return output\\n\\n\\ndef _save_5method_format(all_pairs, n_gens, exp_dir, analysis_dir):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Save lead-lag results in the format expected by MMCT module.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    pairs_out = []\\n    for p in all_pairs:\\n        r = p[\\\"results\\\"]\\n        pair_entry = {\\n            \\\"canary\\\": p[\\\"canary\\\"],\\n            \\\"downstream\\\": p[\\\"downstream\\\"],\\n            \\\"forward\\\": {},\\n            \\\"reverse\\\": {},\\n        }\\n        for method in [\\\"naive_xcorr\\\", \\\"diff_xcorr\\\", \\\"threshold_onset\\\", \\\"ty_granger\\\"]:\\n            if method in r:\\n                pair_entry[\\\"forward\\\"][method] = r[method]\\n        if \\\"perm_granger_fwd\\\" in r:\\n            pair_entry[\\\"forward\\\"][\\\"perm_granger\\\"] = r[\\\"perm_granger_fwd\\\"]\\n        if \\\"perm_granger_rev\\\" in r:\\n            pair_entry[\\\"reverse\\\"][\\\"perm_granger\\\"] = r[\\\"perm_granger_rev\\\"]\\n        pairs_out.append(pair_entry)\\n\\n    output = {\\n        \\\"n_generations\\\": n_gens,\\n        \\\"data_dir\\\": exp_dir,\\n        \\\"methods\\\": [\\\"naive_xcorr\\\", \\\"diff_xcorr\\\", \\\"threshold_onset\\\",\\n                     \\\"perm_granger\\\", \\\"ty_granger\\\"],\\n        \\\"pairs\\\": pairs_out,\\n    }\\n    path = os.path.join(analysis_dir, \\\"5method_leadlag.json\\\")\\n    with open(path, \\\"w\\\") as f:\\n        json.dump(_clean(output), f, indent=2)\\n\\n\\ndef _clean(obj):\\n    if isinstance(obj, (np.floating, np.float64, np.float32)):\\n        return float(obj)\\n    if isinstance(obj, (np.integer,)):\\n        return int(obj)\\n    if isinstance(obj, np.bool_):\\n        return bool(obj)\\n    if isinstance(obj, dict):\\n        return {k: _clean(v) for k, v in obj.items()}\\n    if isinstance(obj, list):\\n        return [_clean(v) for v in obj]\\n    if isinstance(obj, np.ndarray):\\n        return [_clean(v) for v in obj.tolist()]\\n    return obj\\n\\n\\nif __name__ == \\\"__main__\\\":\\n    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=\\\"Batch lead-lag analysis\\\")\\n    parser.add_argument(\\\"--exp_dir\\\", required=True,\\n                        help=\\\"Experiment results directory with all_metrics.json\\\")\\n    parser.add_argument(\\\"--output\\\", default=None,\\n                        help=\\\"Output JSON path (default: {exp_dir}/analysis_results.json)\\\")\\n    parser.add_argument(\\\"--null_dirs\\\", nargs=\\\"+\\\", default=None,\\n                        help=\\\"Alpha=0.00 directories for full MMCT null distribution\\\")\\n    args = prev = 1.0\n    for rank_idx in range(n - 1, -1, -1):\n        orig_idx, p = indexed[rank_idx]\n        rank = rank_idx + 1\n        adj_p = min(prev, p * n / rank)\n        adjusted[orig_idx] =               'thresh_co': None, 'thresh_do': None, 'thresh_lt': None, 'thresh_sig': False,\n                'permG_sig_fdr': False,\n                'ty_pval': 1.0, 'ty_sig_raw': False, 'ty_chi2': None,\n            }\n            \n            if pair_key in pair_lookup:\n                r = pair_lookup[pair_key]\n                naive = r.get('naive_xcorr', {})\n                pr['naive_peak_lag'] = naive.get('peak_lag')\n                pr['naive_sig'] = naive.get('significant', False)\n                \n                diff = r.get('diff_xcorr', {})\n                pr['diff_peak_lag'] = diff.get('peak_lag')\n                pr['diff_sig_fdr'] = diff.get('significant_fdr', diff.get('significant', False))\n                \n                pg = [results[k]['ty_pval'] if results[k]['ty_pval'] is not None else 1.0 for k in pair_order]\n    adj_pvals, sig_fdr = bh_fdr(ty_pvals)\n    for i, k in enumerate(pair_order):\n        results[k]['ty_pfdr'] = adj_pvals[i]\n        results[k]['ty_sig_fdr'] = sig_fdr[i]\n    \n    counts = {\n        'naive': sum(results[k]['naive_fwd'] for k in pair_order),\n        'diff':
